Model before rewriting:

find x: int(5..7)
find y: int(0..3)
find z: int(0..4)

such that

x % y % z != 3

--

x
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
x
new variables:
  find x#direct_1: bool
  find x#direct_2: bool
  find x#direct_3: bool
new constraints:
  sum([toInt(x#direct_1),toInt(x#direct_2),toInt(x#direct_3);int(1..)]) <= 1
  or([x#direct_1,x#direct_2,x#direct_3;int(1..)])

--

y
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
y
new variables:
  find y#direct_1: bool
  find y#direct_2: bool
  find y#direct_3: bool
  find y#direct_4: bool
new constraints:
  sum([toInt(y#direct_1),toInt(y#direct_2),toInt(y#direct_3),toInt(y#direct_4);int(1..)]) <= 1
  or([y#direct_1,y#direct_2,y#direct_3,y#direct_4;int(1..)])

--

z
   ~~> select_representation ([("ReprGeneral", 10000)])
z
new variables:
  find z#direct_1: bool
  find z#direct_2: bool
  find z#direct_3: bool
  find z#direct_4: bool
  find z#direct_5: bool
new constraints:
  sum([toInt(z#direct_1),toInt(z#direct_2),toInt(z#direct_3),toInt(z#direct_4),toInt(z#direct_5);int(1..)]) <= 1
  or([z#direct_1,z#direct_2,z#direct_3,z#direct_4,z#direct_5;int(1..)])

--

x % y % z != 3,
sum([toInt(x#direct_1),toInt(x#direct_2),toInt(x#direct_3);int(1..)]) <= 1,
or([x#direct_1,x#direct_2,x#direct_3;int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(y#direct_1),toInt(y#direct_2),toInt(y#direct_3),toInt(y#direct_4);int(1..)]) <= 1,
or([y#direct_1,y#direct_2,y#direct_3,y#direct_4;int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(z#direct_1),toInt(z#direct_2),toInt(z#direct_3),toInt(z#direct_4),toInt(z#direct_5);int(1..)]) <= 1,
or([z#direct_1,z#direct_2,z#direct_3,z#direct_4,z#direct_5;int(1..)])
   ~~> select_cardinality ([("SAT", 20000)])
x % y % z != 3,
or([x#direct_1,x#direct_2,x#direct_3;int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(y#direct_1),toInt(y#direct_2),toInt(y#direct_3),toInt(y#direct_4);int(1..)]) <= 1,
or([y#direct_1,y#direct_2,y#direct_3,y#direct_4;int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(z#direct_1),toInt(z#direct_2),toInt(z#direct_3),toInt(z#direct_4),toInt(z#direct_5);int(1..)]) <= 1,
or([z#direct_1,z#direct_2,z#direct_3,z#direct_4,z#direct_5;int(1..)])
new SAT encoding decisions:
  at-most-one(x#direct_1, x#direct_2, x#direct_3) using None

--

x % y % z != 3,
or([x#direct_1,x#direct_2,x#direct_3;int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(y#direct_1),toInt(y#direct_2),toInt(y#direct_3),toInt(y#direct_4);int(1..)]) <= 1,
or([y#direct_1,y#direct_2,y#direct_3,y#direct_4;int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(z#direct_1),toInt(z#direct_2),toInt(z#direct_3),toInt(z#direct_4),toInt(z#direct_5);int(1..)]) <= 1,
or([z#direct_1,z#direct_2,z#direct_3,z#direct_4,z#direct_5;int(1..)])
   ~~> select_cardinality ([("SAT", 20000)])
x % y % z != 3,
or([x#direct_1,x#direct_2,x#direct_3;int(1..)]),
or([y#direct_1,y#direct_2,y#direct_3,y#direct_4;int(1..)]),
sum([toInt(z#direct_1),toInt(z#direct_2),toInt(z#direct_3),toInt(z#direct_4),toInt(z#direct_5);int(1..)]) <= 1,
or([z#direct_1,z#direct_2,z#direct_3,z#direct_4,z#direct_5;int(1..)])
new SAT encoding decisions:
  at-most-one(y#direct_1, y#direct_2, y#direct_3, y#direct_4) using None

--

   ~~> select_cardinality ([("SAT", 20000)])
x % y % z != 3,

or([y#direct_1,y#direct_2,y#direct_3,y#direct_4;int(1..)]),
